Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The quarks operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 434. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 579 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_434(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 434. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^579(S, F) is trivial. QED.
